    def require_isolated_clipboard(
        kind: str,
        environ: Optional[dict] = None,
        platform: Optional[str] = None,
        account: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Fail the test (never skip) before anything opens a clipboard.

        Summary:
            The isolation guard of this module's helpers: calls
            :func:`clipboard_isolated` and ``pytest.fail``s with
            ``OS clipboard not isolated: <reason>`` when it is not isolated. It
            opens nothing itself; every clipboard helper of this module calls it
            first.

        Args:
            kind (str): ``"windows"`` or ``"x"``.
            environ (Optional[dict]): Passed through to :func:`clipboard_isolated`.
            platform (Optional[str]): Passed through.
            account (Optional[str]): Passed through.
        """
        ok, reason = clipboard_isolated(kind, environ, platform, account)
        if not ok:
>           pytest.fail(f"OS clipboard not isolated: {reason}")

tests\support\b103_clipboard.py:179: Failed
================================== FAILURES ===================================
_ test_tc_b100_20_writers_on_a_31_folder_manifest_finish_under_one_second[variant-append] _

tmp_path = WindowsPath('[USER_HOME]/Github/s19_app-b103-g3-local-out/tmp/pytest-of-[ACCOUNT]/pytest-1/test_tc_b100_20_writers_on_a_30')
writer = 'variant-append'

    @pytest.mark.serial_timing
    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "writer", ["variant-append", "mac-sync", "a2l-sync", "save-back", "save-project"]
    )
    def test_tc_b100_20_writers_on_a_31_folder_manifest_finish_under_one_second(
        tmp_path: Path, writer: str
    ) -> None:
        """Inc 7 corrective pass 2, SEC7-R2-2 (SEC6-5's time bound for the
        writers; security probe T2): P records 1 000 real files at the end of a
        31-folder chain in ``batch``, ``assignments`` and ``gathered`` (no
        finding, so every writer rewrites). Each in-project writer � variant
        append, MAC sync, A2L sync, save-back � and Save project with that
        composition reach their verified manifest line under 1 s from the
        trigger.

        RED (cp1 tree): append 6.89 s, MAC sync 7.12 s, A2L sync 6.44 s,
        save-back 6.42 s (one fresh walk per entry in the serializer).

        Fix round 2 R-1 (measurement re-pin, the Inc 12b F-23 rule): the loads
        (Inc 12a) and Save project (Inc 12b) run on workers, so the polling
        loop saw the outcome line some time after the app painted it. The clock
        now stops when ``set_status`` receives the outcome line (the bound's end
        point: the line is painted by the main-thread commit), not when the
        settle loop notices it.

        Batch-101 Inc 3: the ``save-project`` parameter verifies R-PROJ-001.13;
        its 0.8 s target is read from the QC-TM row, not asserted."""
        from s19_app.tui.app import SaveProjectPayload

        base = tmp_path / "app"
        project_dir = projects.seed_project_with_every_key(_workarea(base))
        names = projects.real_files_under_a_chain(project_dir)
        manifest = projects.read_manifest(project_dir)
        manifest.update(batch=names, assignments={"firmware": names}, gathered={"patches": names})
        (project_dir / "project.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(manifest, separators=(",", ":")), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        sources = {
            "variant-append": (projects.CASE02_DIR, "firmware.s19"),
            "mac-sync": (projects.CASE01_DIR, "firmware.mac"),
            "a2l-sync": (projects.CASE01_DIR, "firmware.a2l"),
        }
        src = None
        if writer in sources:
            folder, name = sources[writer]
            src = projects.copy_sources(tmp_path / "src", folder, name) / name
        statuses: list = []
        painted_at: list = []

        async def _drive() -> float:
            app = S19TuiApp(base_dir=base)
            real_set_status = app.set_status

            def _capture(message, *args, **kwargs):
                statuses.append(str(message))
                if not painted_at and any(o in str(message) for o in WRITER_OUTCOMES):
                    painted_at.append(time.perf_counter())
                return real_set_status(message, *args, **kwargs)

            app.set_status = _capture
            async with app.run_test() as pilot:
                await pilot.pause()
                await _open_seeded_project(pilot, app)
                statuses.clear()
                painted_at.clear()
                started = time.perf_counter()
                if writer == "save-back":
                    _apply_patch(app)
                    app.on_patch_editor_panel_save_back_decision(
                        PatchEditorPanel.SaveBackDecision("firmware.s19", 32, output_format="s19")
                    )
                elif writer == "variant-append" or writer == "mac-sync":
                    app.load_from_path(src)
                elif writer == "a2l-sync":
                    app.load_a2l_from_path(src)
                else:
                    app._handle_save_dialog(
                        SaveProjectPayload(
                            parent_folder=str(app.workarea),
                            project_name="P",
                            batch=tuple(names),
                            assignments={"firmware": tuple(names)},
                        )
                    )
                await _wait_yielding(
                    pilot,
                    lambda: any(o in s for s in statuses for o in WRITER_OUTCOMES),
                    f"the {writer}'s manifest outcome",
                )
                return painted_at[0] - started

        took = asyncio.run(_drive())
        outcomes = [s for s in statuses if any(o in s for o in WRITER_OUTCOMES)]
        print(f"{writer}: trigger -> outcome {took:.3f} s; {outcomes[:1]}")
        assert "manifest verified" in outcomes[0], outcomes

    def test_tc_b100_20_writers_on_a_31_folder_manifest_finish_under_one_second(
        tmp_path: Path, writer: str
    ) -> None:
        """Inc 7 corrective pass 2, SEC7-R2-2 (SEC6-5's time bound for the
        writers; security probe T2): P records 1 000 real files at the end of a
        31-folder chain in ``batch``, ``assignments`` and ``gathered`` (no
        finding, so every writer rewrites). Each in-project writer � variant
        append, MAC sync, A2L sync, save-back � and Save project with that
        composition reach their verified manifest line under 1 s from the
        trigger.

        RED (cp1 tree): append 6.89 s, MAC sync 7.12 s, A2L sync 6.44 s,
        save-back 6.42 s (one fresh walk per entry in the serializer).

        Fix round 2 R-1 (measurement re-pin, the Inc 12b F-23 rule): the loads
        (Inc 12a) and Save project (Inc 12b) run on workers, so the polling
        loop saw the outcome line some time after the app painted it. The clock
        now stops when ``set_status`` receives the outcome line (the bound's end
        point: the line is painted by the main-thread commit), not when the
        settle loop notices it.

        Batch-101 Inc 3: the ``save-project`` parameter verifies R-PROJ-001.13;
        its 0.8 s target is read from the QC-TM row, not asserted."""
        from s19_app.tui.app import SaveProjectPayload

        base = tmp_path / "app"
        project_dir = projects.seed_project_with_every_key(_workarea(base))
        names = projects.real_files_under_a_chain(project_dir)
        manifest = projects.read_manifest(project_dir)
        manifest.update(batch=names, assignments={"firmware": names}, gathered={"patches": names})
        (project_dir / "project.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(manifest, separators=(",", ":")), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        sources = {
            "variant-append": (projects.CASE02_DIR, "firmware.s19"),
            "mac-sync": (projects.CASE01_DIR, "firmware.mac"),
            "a2l-sync": (projects.CASE01_DIR, "firmware.a2l"),
        }
        src = None
        if writer in sources:
            folder, name = sources[writer]
            src = projects.copy_sources(tmp_path / "src", folder, name) / name
        statuses: list = []
        painted_at: list = []

        async def _drive() -> float:
            app = S19TuiApp(base_dir=base)
            real_set_status = app.set_status

            def _capture(message, *args, **kwargs):
                statuses.append(str(message))
                if not painted_at and any(o in str(message) for o in WRITER_OUTCOMES):
                    painted_at.append(time.perf_counter())
                return real_set_status(message, *args, **kwargs)

            app.set_status = _capture
            async with app.run_test() as pilot:
                await pilot.pause()
                await _open_seeded_project(pilot, app)
                statuses.clear()
                painted_at.clear()
                started = time.perf_counter()
                if writer == "save-back":
                    _apply_patch(app)
                    app.on_patch_editor_panel_save_back_decision(
                        PatchEditorPanel.SaveBackDecision("firmware.s19", 32, output_format="s19")
                    )
                elif writer == "variant-append" or writer == "mac-sync":
                    app.load_from_path(src)
                elif writer == "a2l-sync":
                    app.load_a2l_from_path(src)
                else:
                    app._handle_save_dialog(
                        SaveProjectPayload(
                            parent_folder=str(app.workarea),
                            project_name="P",
                            batch=tuple(names),
                            assignments={"firmware": tuple(names)},
                        )
                    )
                await _wait_yielding(
                    pilot,
                    lambda: any(o in s for s in statuses for o in WRITER_OUTCOMES),
                    f"the {writer}'s manifest outcome",
                )
                return painted_at[0] - started

        took = asyncio.run(_drive())
        outcomes = [s for s in statuses if any(o in s for o in WRITER_OUTCOMES)]
        print(f"{writer}: trigger -> outcome {took:.3f} s; {outcomes[:1]}")
        assert "manifest verified" in outcomes[0], outcomes
        if writer == "save-project":
            # R-PROJ-001.13 fixes the exact line, not a line that holds it.
            assert outcomes[0] == "Project saved + manifest verified", outcomes

        """Inc 7 corrective pass 2 (R2-2), the folder ceiling's worst case: 3 x

    def _assert_sec6_5_bounded(scene: dict) -> None:
        timings = {key: scene[key] for key in ("read", "load", "painted", "appended")}
        print("SEC6-5 timings (s):", {key: round(value, 3) for key, value in timings.items()})
        assert scene["read"] < report_margin("sec6_5 read", scene["read"], timing_bound(1.0)), timings

    @pytest.mark.serial_timing
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scene", ["all-bad", "cap", "scene"])
    def test_r_tst_111_112_arm_values_equal_the_product_stamps(
        monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: Path, scene: str
    ) -> None:
        """R-TST-111 and R-TST-112: each timing arm's recorded panel / append
        value equals the difference of the product's own stamps, read by an
        observer that shares nothing with the arm. The arm runs once; its
        ``report_margin`` calls are
        recorded (and still forwarded, so the margin rows are kept).

        ``all-bad`` / ``cap``: the arm's ``painted`` equals observer paint stamp
        minus observer install stamp. ``scene``: ``sec6_5 painted`` equals the
        same difference and ``sec6_5 appended`` equals the observer's outcome
        stamp minus the observer's ``load_from_path`` entry. Equality is within
        ``_STAMP_TOLERANCE_S`` (``math.isclose``), not an ordering assert.

        Why: a clock that stops when a poll notices the paint reads 0.5-0.7 s
        too long; only a comparison against an independent reading can fail on
        that, and it fails the same way for any later arm that goes back to a
        poll."""
        module = sys.modules[__name__]
        real_report_margin = report_margin
        recorded: list = []

        def _record(label, measured, bound):
            recorded.append((label, measured))
            return real_report_margin(label, measured, bound)

        monkeypatch.setattr(module, "report_margin", _record)
        observer = _StampObserver.install(monkeypatch)
        if scene == "all-bad":
            test_tc_b100_61_every_array_at_the_cap_all_bad_opens_under_one_second(tmp_path)
            panel_label, append_label = "TC-B100-61 all-bad painted", None
        elif scene == "cap":

tests\test_b100_project_check.py:1712:
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

tmp_path = WindowsPath('[USER_HOME]/Github/s19_app-b103-g3-local-out/tmp/pytest-of-[ACCOUNT]/pytest-1/test_r_tst_111_112_arm_values_1')

    @pytest.mark.serial_timing
    def test_tc_b100_61_files_at_the_cap_all_missing_are_bounded(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        """Inc 7 corrective F2 (reviewer probe PB): ``files`` holds the image's
        record plus 999 well-formed records for files not on disk (exactly the
        cap: not damaged) \u2192 999 ``missing`` problems; the panel lists 102 of
        them and ONE row counting the other 897, paints under 1 s, and the
        summary line still counts all 999.

        RED (corrective-pass base): 999 panel rows, painted in 2.45 s."""
        base = tmp_path / "app"
        project_dir = projects.copy_sources(_workarea(base) / "P", projects.CASE01_DIR, "firmware.s19")
        _write_manifest(
            project_dir, hostile.dumps(hostile.files_at_cap_missing(projects.file_records(project_dir)))
        )

        async def _drive() -> tuple:
            app = S19TuiApp(base_dir=base)
            real_load = app._handle_load_project
            timing: list = []

            def _timed(name, *args, **kwargs):  # Inc 12b X-6: the router passes routed=True
                app._load_started_at = time.perf_counter()
                real_load(name, *args, **kwargs)

            # E4 integration re-pin (A-8): at 12b the handler only starts the
            # worker, so the load is timed to the end of the one install callback
            # (the work the base did inside the handler), and the panel's paint
            # clock starts there, as it did at the base.
            real_install = app._install_prepared_project

            def _installed(prepared):
                real_install(prepared)
                timing.append(time.perf_counter() - app._load_started_at)
                app._load_returned_at = time.perf_counter()

            app._handle_load_project = _timed
            app._install_prepared_project = _installed
            stamps = _watch_panel_paint(app)
            async with app.run_test(size=(118, 54)) as pilot:
                await pilot.pause()
                await _open_panel_through_the_dialog(pilot, app)
                pressed = time.perf_counter()
                await wait_until(
                    pilot,
                    lambda: len(app.screen.query("#result_summary")) > 0
                    and app.screen.query_one("#result_summary").size.height > 0,
                    "timed out waiting for the painted Load-result panel",
                    timeout=60.0,
                )
                assert stamps, "the Load-result panel was never laid out with a summary"
                painted = stamps[0][0] - _paint_clock_start(app, pressed)
                rows = len(app.screen.query("#result_body .result-problem"))
                assert stamps[0][1] == rows, (
                    f"the stamped layout held {stamps[0][1]} problem rows; the panel ends with {rows}"
                )
                title = str(app.screen.query_one(".modal-title", Label).render())
                summary = [line for line in app.log_lines if line.endswith("details: p")]
                return timing, painted, rows, app._last_load_announcement, title, summary

        timing, painted, rows, announcement, title, summary = asyncio.run(_drive())

    @pytest.mark.serial_timing
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scene", ["all-bad", "cap", "scene"])
    def test_r_tst_111_112_arm_values_equal_the_product_stamps(
        monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: Path, scene: str
    ) -> None:
        """R-TST-111 and R-TST-112: each timing arm's recorded panel / append
        value equals the difference of the product's own stamps, read by an
        observer that shares nothing with the arm. The arm runs once; its
        ``report_margin`` calls are
        recorded (and still forwarded, so the margin rows are kept).

        ``all-bad`` / ``cap``: the arm's ``painted`` equals observer paint stamp
        minus observer install stamp. ``scene``: ``sec6_5 painted`` equals the
        same difference and ``sec6_5 appended`` equals the observer's outcome
        stamp minus the observer's ``load_from_path`` entry. Equality is within
        ``_STAMP_TOLERANCE_S`` (``math.isclose``), not an ordering assert.

        Why: a clock that stops when a poll notices the paint reads 0.5-0.7 s
        too long; only a comparison against an independent reading can fail on
        that, and it fails the same way for any later arm that goes back to a
        poll."""
        module = sys.modules[__name__]
        real_report_margin = report_margin
        recorded: list = []

        def _record(label, measured, bound):
            recorded.append((label, measured))
            return real_report_margin(label, measured, bound)

        monkeypatch.setattr(module, "report_margin", _record)
        observer = _StampObserver.install(monkeypatch)
        if scene == "all-bad":
            test_tc_b100_61_every_array_at_the_cap_all_bad_opens_under_one_second(tmp_path)
            panel_label, append_label = "TC-B100-61 all-bad painted", None
        elif scene == "cap":
            test_tc_b100_61_files_at_the_cap_all_missing_are_bounded(tmp_path)
            panel_label, append_label = "TC-B100-61 cap painted", None
        else:
>           _assert_sec6_5_bounded(_distinct_folders_scene(tmp_path))

____________ test_qc_tm_10b_1_a2l_refresh_cost_on_the_largest_a2l _____________

tmp_path = WindowsPath('[USER_HOME]/Github/s19_app-b103-g3-local-out/tmp/pytest-of-[ACCOUNT]/pytest-1/test_qc_tm_10b_1_a2l_refresh_c0')

    @pytest.mark.serial_timing
    def test_qc_tm_10b_1_a2l_refresh_cost_on_the_largest_a2l(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        """QC-TM-10b-1: the UI-thread time of one in-place write's A2L refresh
        (``_refresh_a2l_values_after_write``) on the largest A2L the repo ships
        (case_06, re-pointed at E5 R-1) stays under ``timing_bound(1.0)``; the tag
        count is in the QC-TM label.

        The refresh is timed where the write notice calls it (the method wrapped on
        the app instance, one real apply of the complement of the byte at
        ``0x80100005`` through the Patch Editor). After the bound, the enriched
        tags must equal a full recompute over the written image (the partial
        recompute of R-1 must describe the same bytes). Negative controls: the full refresh at
        every write -> above the bound -> RED; a changed-byte test that reads only
        a tag's first byte -> the tags covering ``0x80100005`` keep their old
        values -> RED."""
        from s19_app.tui.services.a2l_service import enrich_tags_and_render

        async def scenario(app, pilot, log):
            await load_image(pilot, app, CASE06 / "firmware.s19", timeout=240.0)
            await load_a2l(pilot, app, CASE06 / "firmware.a2l", timeout=240.0)
            await wait_until(
                pilot,
                lambda: app._a2l_enriched_key is not None and len(app._a2l_enriched_tags) > 0,
                "the case_06 tags were never enriched",
                timeout=240.0,
            )
            assert hasattr(app, "_refresh_a2l_values_after_write"), (
                "the write notice has no A2L refresh to time"
            )
            real = app._refresh_a2l_values_after_write
            spans: list = []

            def timed(*args, **kwargs):
                start = time.perf_counter()
                try:
                    return real(*args, **kwargs)
                finally:
                    spans.append(time.perf_counter() - start)

            app._refresh_a2l_values_after_write = timed
            tags = len(app._a2l_enriched_tags)
            old = mem_at(app, CASE06_WRITE)
            assert old is not None, "0x80100005 is not in the case_06 image"
            await patch_apply(
                pilot, app, log, tmp_path / "docs", "d.json", [(CASE06_WRITE, bytes([old ^ 0xFF]))]
            )
            assert mem_at(app, CASE06_WRITE) == old ^ 0xFF, "the apply did not write"
            await wait_until(
                pilot,
                lambda: bool(spans),
                "the write notice never ran the A2L refresh",
                timeout=240.0,
            )
            elapsed = spans[0]
            print(f"QC-TM-10b-1 case_06: {tags} tags, {elapsed:.3f} s")
            assert elapsed < report_margin(
                f"QC-TM-10b-1 A2L refresh {tags} tags", elapsed, timing_bound(1.0)
            ), f"{tags} tags: {elapsed:.3f} s"
            full, _lines = enrich_tags_and_render(
                app.current_a2l_data, app.current_file.mem_map, max_tag_lines=500
            )
            differ = [
                i for i, (got, want) in enumerate(zip(app._a2l_enriched_tags, full)) if got != want
            ]
            assert len(app._a2l_enriched_tags) == len(full) and not differ, (
                f"{len(differ)} enriched tags differ from a full recompute "
                f"(first {differ[:3]}; {len(app._a2l_enriched_tags)} vs {len(full)} tags)"
            )

>       drive(tmp_path, scenario)

                 check: Callable[[int, dict], None],
                 image_of: Optional[Callable[[str], dict]] = None) -> None:
        """The E3S3-24 measure for one case: absolute bound at S (least of 15 runs)
        then the interleaved least-of-15 per-object ratio at 2S and 8S."""

                                          min(runs), timing_bound(ABS_BOUND_S)), runs

        results = {}

    def test_r_a2l_def_150_arr_10_element_decode_does_not_grow_with_name(tmp_path, mode):
        """EN-1 (``enum``: TAB_INTP with an undeclared table, one refusal per element)
        and EN-2 (``number``: NO_COMPU_METHOD): the decode time per element does not
        grow with the object's name (S / 2S / 8S, interleaved, least of 15)."""
        def build(p, kib):
            name = "V" + "N" * (kib * 1024)
            if mode == "enum":
                body = _cm("CM_I", 'TAB_INTP "%.3" ""', "COMPU_TAB_REF NOT_DECLARED") + \
                    _valblk(name, "0x1000", "RL_U8", "CM_I", "0", "255", "NUMBER 64")
            else:
                body = _valblk(name, "0x1000", "RL_U8", "NO_COMPU_METHOD", "0", "255", "NUMBER 64")
            return _parse(p, body), [name]

        loaded = {kib: build(tmp_path, kib) for kib in SIZES_KIB}
        part = {kib: float("inf") for kib in SIZES_KIB}
        for _ in range(LEAST_OF):

                a2l_data, names = loaded[kib]
                defs = a2ldef.describe_definitions(names, a2l_data)
                image = img(0x1000, bytes([250]) * 64)

                def work():
                    a2ldef.decode_values(defs[names[0]], a2l_data, image)

    def _gp2_run(tmp_path: Path, case: str, label: str, work: Callable[[_Loaded], Any],
                 check: Callable[[int, _Loaded, Any], None], image_byte: Optional[int] = None,
                 steady: Optional[Callable[[_Loaded], tuple]] = None) -> None:
        """Absolute bound at S (400 calls within 0.5 s, wall clock, the least of 7 runs) then the scaling bound.

        A describe case passes ``steady`` (E5-3): it returns (the CPU interval of the 399 names after the first, read
        inside one fresh walk, that walk's result) and the per-object part is the least of >= 15 interleaved rounds of
        that interval.  A decode case keeps one interval: the process CPU time of ``work`` itself."""
        loaded = {kib: _gp2_load(tmp_path, case, kib, image_byte) for kib in SIZES_KIB}
        runs, result = [], None
        for _ in range(LEAST_OF):
            seconds, result = _timed(lambda: work(loaded[SIZE_S_KIB]))
            runs.append(seconds)
            if seconds > HOPELESS_S:
                break
        least = min(runs)
        assert least <= report_margin(f"R-A2L-DEF.{label} 400 calls at 256 KiB", least, timing_bound(ABS_BOUND_S)), runs
        check(SIZE_S_KIB, loaded[SIZE_S_KIB], result)

        part = {kib: float("inf") for kib in SIZES_KIB}
        results = {}
        for _ in range(SCALE_ROUNDS):
            slow = False
            for kib in SIZES_KIB:                    # interleaved: S, 2S, 8S in turn within every round
                if steady is not None:
                    t_outer, (interval, results[kib]) = _timed(lambda: steady(loaded[kib]), time.process_time)
                else:
                    t_outer, results[kib] = _timed(lambda: work(loaded[kib]), time.process_time)
                    interval = t_outer
                part[kib] = min(part[kib], interval)
                slow = slow or t_outer > SLOW_RUN_S
            if slow:
                break
        if steady is not None:
>           assert part[SIZE_S_KIB] >= NON_VACUITY_S, part

image_byte = None, steady = <function _describe_steady at 0x000002631FC984A0>

    def _gp2_run(tmp_path: Path, case: str, label: str, work: Callable[[_Loaded], Any],
                 check: Callable[[int, _Loaded, Any], None], image_byte: Optional[int] = None,
                 steady: Optional[Callable[[_Loaded], tuple]] = None) -> None:
        """Absolute bound at S (400 calls within 0.5 s, wall clock, the least of 7 runs) then the scaling bound.

        A describe case passes ``steady`` (E5-3): it returns (the CPU interval of the 399 names after the first, read
        inside one fresh walk, that walk's result) and the per-object part is the least of >= 15 interleaved rounds of
        that interval.  A decode case keeps one interval: the process CPU time of ``work`` itself."""
        loaded = {kib: _gp2_load(tmp_path, case, kib, image_byte) for kib in SIZES_KIB}
        runs, result = [], None
        for _ in range(LEAST_OF):
            seconds, result = _timed(lambda: work(loaded[SIZE_S_KIB]))
            runs.append(seconds)
            if seconds > HOPELESS_S:
                break
        least = min(runs)
        assert least <= report_margin(f"R-A2L-DEF.{label} 400 calls at 256 KiB", least, timing_bound(ABS_BOUND_S)), runs
        check(SIZE_S_KIB, loaded[SIZE_S_KIB], result)

        part = {kib: float("inf") for kib in SIZES_KIB}
        results = {}
        for _ in range(SCALE_ROUNDS):
            slow = False
            for kib in SIZES_KIB:                    # interleaved: S, 2S, 8S in turn within every round
                if steady is not None:
                    t_outer, (interval, results[kib]) = _timed(lambda: steady(loaded[kib]), time.process_time)
                else:
                    t_outer, results[kib] = _timed(lambda: work(loaded[kib]), time.process_time)
                    interval = t_outer
                part[kib] = min(part[kib], interval)
                slow = slow or t_outer > SLOW_RUN_S
            if slow:
                break
        if steady is not None:
>           assert part[SIZE_S_KIB] >= NON_VACUITY_S, part
E           AssertionError: {256: 0.0, 512: 0.0, 2048: 0.0}
E           assert 0.0 >= 0.002

    @pytest.mark.serial_timing
    def test_r_a2l_list_035_1(tmp_path) -> None:
        """R-A2L-LIST.035/1: a 2,048-name list over 13,990 tags paints within 1.0 s of the debounce (median of 3)."""
        import shutil

        from tests.support.b103_label_list import build_paste, case_names

        folder = tmp_path / "swa"
        folder.mkdir()
        shutil.copy(REPO_ROOT / "examples" / "case_00_public" / "software_a.a2l", folder / "firmware.a2l")
        text = build_paste(case_names(folder)).replace("\n", ", ")

        async def sc(app, pilot):
            box = app.query_one("#a2l_tags_filter_input", Input)
            times = []
            for _ in range(3):
                box.value = ""
                await flush(app, pilot)
                start = time.perf_counter()
                box.value = text
                await wait_until(
                    pilot, lambda: chips(app)[0].startswith(" LIST ") and missing_text(app).startswith("Missing "),
                    "the list-mode table, counts and missing lines", timeout=30,
                )
                times.append(time.perf_counter() - start - 0.15)
            return statistics.median(times), len(app._a2l_enriched_tags)

    def require_isolated_clipboard(
        kind: str,
        environ: Optional[dict] = None,
        platform: Optional[str] = None,
        account: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Fail the test (never skip) before anything opens a clipboard.

        Summary:
            The isolation guard of this module's helpers: calls
            :func:`clipboard_isolated` and ``pytest.fail``s with
            ``OS clipboard not isolated: <reason>`` when it is not isolated. It
            opens nothing itself; every clipboard helper of this module calls it
            first.

        Args:
            kind (str): ``"windows"`` or ``"x"``.
            environ (Optional[dict]): Passed through to :func:`clipboard_isolated`.
            platform (Optional[str]): Passed through.
            account (Optional[str]): Passed through.
        """
        ok, reason = clipboard_isolated(kind, environ, platform, account)
        if not ok:
>           pytest.fail(f"OS clipboard not isolated: {reason}")

_ test_r_pe_bench_94_4_a_queued_decode_shows_its_cue_while_another_rows_summary_runs _

tmp_path = WindowsPath('[USER_HOME]/Github/s19_app-b103-g3-local-out/tmp/pytest-of-[ACCOUNT]/pytest-1/test_r_pe_bench_94_4_a_queued_0')
defc_dir = WindowsPath('[USER_HOME]/Github/s19_app-b103-g3-local-out/tmp/pytest-of-[ACCOUNT]/pytest-1/b103_inc9_w_defc3')
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x00000263547DB440>

    @pytest.mark.serial_timing
    def test_r_pe_bench_94_4_a_queued_decode_shows_its_cue_while_another_rows_summary_runs(tmp_path, defc_dir, monkeypatch):
        """R-PE-BENCH.94/4, EX-BC2: LARGE is selected while another row's summary decode is gated running, so LARGE's own
        decode has not started: the cue still shows `decode LARGE`, within 0.5 s of the ask. Operator need (P-127): the
        window that waits for a queued decode must still say it is working. Negative control: a cue keyed on the running
        job shows nothing for the queued row."""
        a2l, s19 = case(tmp_path, "LARGE", "MID", "ZZZ")
        gate = Gate(monkeypatch, "decode_values")

        async def body(app, pilot, panel):
            sheet = sheet_of(panel)
            prime(app)
            await decoded(pilot, sheet, "LARGE", "MID", "ZZZ")
            stamps = Stamps(sheet)
            try:
                # a page turn that drops LARGE and MID: both are new on the next page, their summaries are held
                definitions = describe_definitions(["ZZZ"], app.current_a2l_data)
                sheet.show_page(["ZZZ"], definitions, app.current_a2l_data, app.current_file.mem_map)
                await pilot.pause(0.2)
                gate.hold.update({"LARGE", "MID"})
                both = ["LARGE", "MID"]
                sheet.show_page(both, describe_definitions(both, app.current_a2l_data), app.current_a2l_data,
                                app.current_file.mem_map)
                await wait_for(pilot, gate.entered.is_set, "a summary held in its worker", 20)
                running = gate.calls[-1][1]
                target = "MID" if running == "LARGE" else "LARGE"
                assert gate.running == 1 and running in both, (gate.calls, gate.running)
                if sheet.selected_name != target:
                    await pick(pilot, sheet, target)
                asked = stamps.asked.get(("decode", target))
                assert asked is not None, f"selecting {target} did not ask its decode: {sorted(stamps.asked)}"
                deadline = timing_bound(0.5)
                took = await until(pilot, lambda: cue_now(app, "decode", target), max(0.0, asked + deadline - time.monotonic()))
                seen = time.monotonic() - asked
                return took, seen, target, running, top_row(app), border_hex(app), gate.running
            finally:
                gate.release()
                stamps.close()

        took, seen, target, running, row, colour, running_now = run(tmp_path, defc_dir, body, a2l=a2l, image=s19)
        assert took is not None and seen <= report_margin(
            "R-PE-BENCH.94/4 queued decode cue", seen, timing_bound(0.5)
        ), (
>           f"no cue `decode {target}` within 0.5 s of its ask while {running}'s summary ran (running={running_now}, {load()}); "
            f"top border {row!r}, painted corner {colour}"
        )

    @pytest.mark.serial_timing
    def test_r_pe_bench_97_2_the_frames_follow_the_cycle_one_step_per_tick_none_skipped(tmp_path, defc_dir, monkeypatch):
        """R-PE-BENCH.97/2, EX-BC5: while a decode is held 3.5 s the frames painted in the title are \u25d0 \u25d3 \u25d1 \u25d2 in that
        order, cyclically, each change one step, none skipped, and the pending time never goes back. Every compositor
        refresh is read (the app's own paints), not a poll. Operator need (P-127): a spinner that stands still is
        indistinguishable from a hung window. Negative control: the frame fixed or advanced by two."""

                real_refresh()
                seen.append((time.monotonic(), top_row(app)))

            try:
                gate.hold.add("LARGE")
                await reselect(pilot, sheet, "LARGE", "OTHER")
                await cue_shown(pilot, app, "decode", "LARGE")
                screen._compositor_refresh = hooked
                end = time.monotonic() + 3.5
                while time.monotonic() < end:
                    await pilot.pause(0.05)
            finally:
                screen.__dict__.pop("_compositor_refresh", None)
                gate.release()
            return seen

    @pytest.mark.serial_timing
    def test_r_pe_bench_102_1_leaving_the_row_for_a_scalar_returns_the_idle_look_while_the_worker_runs(tmp_path, defc_dir, monkeypatch):
        """R-PE-BENCH.102/1, EX-BC10: VAL_BLK_DEF_0001's decode is held running; `down`/`up` to DISPLAY_0001 (a scalar
        row): the idle look is back within 0.3 s of the key, while the worker is still held. Operator need (P-127): the
        cue describes what the user is looking at; amber over a scalar row says nothing true. Negative control: a cue
        keyed on the running worker stays until the gate opens. The compositor hook timestamps the actual idle paint;
        Pilot's return after its own pause is not the paint time."""
        gate = Gate(monkeypatch, "decode_values")

        async def body(app, pilot, panel):
            sheet = sheet_of(panel)
            screen = app.screen
            real_refresh = screen._compositor_refresh
            paints = []

            def record_paint():
                real_refresh()
                if sheet.selected_name == DISPLAY:
                    paints.append((time.monotonic(), idle_now(app)))

            prime(app)
            await decoded(pilot, sheet, VB)
            try:
                gate.hold.add(VB)
                await reselect(pilot, sheet, VB, DISPLAY)
                await cue_shown(pilot, app, "decode", VB)
                screen._compositor_refresh = record_paint
                key_at = await move_to(pilot, sheet, DISPLAY)
                deadline = key_at + timing_bound(0.3)
                while time.monotonic() < deadline and not any(at >= key_at and idle for at, idle in paints):
                    await pilot.pause(0.01)
                lag = next((at - key_at for at, idle in paints if at >= key_at and idle), None)
                return lag, gate.released.is_set(), gate.running, top_row(app), border_hex(app)
            finally:
                screen.__dict__.pop("_compositor_refresh", None)
                gate.release()

>       lag, released, running, row, colour = run(tmp_path, defc_dir, body)
